# Skills · C · part 33

887 entries, causal-genomics-mediation-analysis to caveman. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **causal-genomics-mediation-analysis**: Decompose total effects into direct and indirect paths through mediators using mediation, CMAverse 4-way, HIMA/HIMA2 high-dimensional, BAMA, two-step / MVMR mediation, or double-ML medDML. Use when testing whether a molecular phenotype (expression, methylation, protein) mediates a treatment-outcome relationship, decomposing exposure-mediator interaction via VanderWeele 4-way, screening high-dimensional EWAS mediators, or running MR-based mediation when sequential ignorability is implausible. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/causal-genomics-mediation-analysis) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **causal-genomics-mendelian-randomization**: Estimate causal effects of an exposure on an outcome from GWAS summary statistics using genetic instruments. Implements IVW (fixed/random), MR-Egger, weighted median/mode, MR-RAPS, CAUSE, GSMR-HEIDI, MR-PRESSO, MVMR, MR-Clust, LCV, and LHC-MR via TwoSampleMR, MendelianRandomization, MR-PRESSO, cause, and lhcMR. Use when testing causal direction between traits, evaluating drug-target effects via cis-pQTL/cis-eQTL, performing multivariable mediation MR, distinguishing causation from correlated horizontal pleiotropy, or producing STROBE-MR-compliant sensitivity batteries. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/causal-genomics-mendelian-randomization) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **causal-genomics-pleiotropy-detection**: Detect and adjust for horizontal pleiotropy in two-sample Mendelian randomization by distinguishing uncorrelated (UHP) from correlated (CHP) pleiotropy and choosing among Egger, MR-PRESSO, MR-RAPS, CAUSE, LHC-MR, LCV, MR-Clust, MR-Mix, and contamination-mixture methods. Use when validating an MR causal claim, running the STROBE-MR sensitivity battery, suspecting a shared heritable confounder, working under weak-instrument or polygenic-exposure regimes, or reconciling discordant estimates across robust methods. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/causal-genomics-pleiotropy-detection) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **causal-genomics-proteome-mr-drug-target**: Runs cis-pQTL Mendelian randomization for drug-target validation using UKB-PPP (Olink), deCODE (SomaScan), Fenland, INTERVAL, ARIC, and FinnGen-PPP proteomes plus colocalization triangulation, phenome-wide on-target adverse-effect scans, cross-platform Olink/SomaScan replication, and PAV (protein-altering variant) sensitivity. Use when nominating or de-risking a drug target from plasma-proteome GWAS, mimicking pharmacological inhibition via cis-pQTL instruments, separating shared-causal from LD-confounded signal under the Schmidt 2020 cis-MR framework, screening on-target adverse phenotypes p… ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/causal-genomics-proteome-mr-drug-target) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **causal-genomics-transcriptome-wide-association**: Performs gene-level association from GWAS summary statistics via genetically predicted tissue expression using FUSION, PrediXcan, S-PrediXcan, S-MultiXcan, UTMOST, MOSTWAS, kTWAS, EpiXcan, TIGAR-V2, and probabilistic fine-mapping with FOCUS and MA-FOCUS. Use when running TWAS from GWAS sumstats, prioritising candidate causal genes from a GWAS lead locus, picking single-tissue vs cross-tissue models, identifying LD-induced TWAS false positives, choosing ancestry-matched prediction weights, fine-mapping co-regulated TWAS hits, or triangulating TWAS with cis-eQTL Mendelian randomization and colo… ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/causal-genomics-transcriptome-wide-association) · ★104 · 2 copies)

Use when the user mentions causal inference, DiD, difference-in-differences, propensity matching, IV, instrumental variable, "estimate the impact of X without an A/B test", quasi-experiment, or natural experiment. ([vermapragya/analytics-skill](https://github.com/vermapragya/analytics-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-inference))
- **causal-inference**: Determine cause-and-effect relationships using propensity scoring, instrumental variables, and causal graphs for policy evaluation and treatment effects ([Xinyu-CN/ft-agent-skills](https://github.com/Xinyu-CN/ft-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skill/aj-geddes--useful-ai-prompts--causal-inference))
- **causal-inference**: Grades a causal sentence on an evidence ladder: explicit contrast and timeline, rival explanations, an intervention or stated identification assumptions, a mechanism chain with recurrence-prevention evidence, and a separate step for responsibility; each rung unlocks its wording — no rung, no word. Use before writing any sentence that says X caused Y, names a root cause, assigns responsibility, or promises that changing X will fix Y, whenever that sentence will lead to a change or point at a person or a team; also before concluding 'it is this one' while debugging. ([zuoxu3310/AGENTOS](https://github.com/zuoxu3310/AGENTOS/tree/HEAD/kernel/causal-inference))
- **causal-inference-advanced**: Use when implementing advanced causal inference in ML. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-inference-advanced) · ★2)
- **causal-inference-advisor**: Guide causal inference from observational or quasi-experimental data. Activate when the user wants to estimate a causal effect, choose between causal methods (DiD, synthetic control, RDD, IV, matching, IPW), construct a DAG, test causal assumptions, run refutation tests, or estimate heterogeneous treatment effects. Covers the full identify-estimate-refute pipeline using DoWhy, EconML, and CausalML. ([WilliamWJHuang/ab-test-causal-inference-skills](https://github.com/WilliamWJHuang/ab-test-causal-inference-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-inference-advisor) · ★4)
- **causal-inference-agent**: Expert agent in Causal Inference, Potential Outcomes (Neyman-Rubin), Causal DAGs (Pearl), SUTVA, Positivity, Ignorability, Propensity Score Matching, IPTW, MSM, AIPW, and Mediation. Grounding Book: Fundamentals of causal inference using R (Brumback). ([minorbonillagomez/NEVEN](https://github.com/minorbonillagomez/NEVEN/tree/HEAD/ontologia-fuentes/LIBROS%20ECONOMETRIA/.agents/skills/causal-inference-agent) · ★3)
- **causal-inference-analysis**: Load when a user asks whether X causes Y, or whether a campaign, policy or treatment caused an outcome, and wants it tested rigorously from their dataset, experiment results or a study's claim, with confounders and assumptions examined. Not for brainstorming possible explanations of an observation (abductive-hypothesis-ranking). ([GSA-TTS/devCrew_s1](https://github.com/GSA-TTS/devCrew_s1/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-inference-analysis) · ★52)
- **causal-inference-analysis**: Use when the user asks to "implement DID", "run difference-in-differences", "set up IV regression", "instrumental variables", "run RDD", "regression discontinuity", "synthetic control", "staggered treatment", "Callaway-Sant'Anna", "Sun-Abraham", "event study", "parallel trends test", "first stage", "Bartik instrument", "shift-share IV", "write Stata regression code", "convert Stata to Python", "use statsmodels", "use linearmodels", "use econml", "panel data regression", "fixed effects", "two-way fixed effects", "cluster standard errors", or any causal inference methodology in Python or Stata. ([HaipingXu/social-science-claude-scholar](https://github.com/HaipingXu/social-science-claude-scholar/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-inference-analysis) · ★26)
- **causal-inference-analysis**: 为观察性或准实验研究规划、审计、估计或解释因果效应；仅在用户明确提出因果效应/识别、反事实、DAG、混杂、倾向得分/匹配/IPW、双重稳健估计、双重差分/事件研究、工具变量、回归不连续、合成控制或目标试验模拟时使用。不要因普通回归、相关分析、预测建模、一般统计检验、A/B 测试或随机实验设计而调用。 ([ranveerankhira-gif/research-agent-skills-zh](https://github.com/ranveerankhira-gif/research-agent-skills-zh/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-inference-analysis))

- **cavecrew-builder**: Surgical 1-2 file edit. Use for typo fixes, single-function rewrites, mechanical renames, comment removal, and format-preserving tweaks. - **cavecrew-investigator**: Read-only code locator. Returns a compact file:line table for "where is X defined", "what calls Y", "list all uses of Z", "map this directory structure". Use whenever the user asks to locate, find, trace, or search for code — definitions, callers, references, imports, or any "where is / where does" question. Refuses to suggest fixes; that's the builder's job. ([khalilgihub/rust_hybrid-player](https://github.com/khalilgihub/rust_hybrid-player/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cavecrew-investigator))
- **cavecrew-reviewer**: Diff/branch/file reviewer. Use for "review this PR", "review my diff", "audit this file", or any request to inspect code for bugs, risks, and correctness issues. One line per finding, severity-tagged, no praise, no scope creep. Skips formatting nits unless they change meaning. ([khalilgihub/rust_hybrid-player](https://github.com/khalilgihub/rust_hybrid-player/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cavecrew-reviewer))

Triggers on any write to SPEC.md or when user says "caveman", "compress this", "be brief". ([pkziinn10/config-opencode](https://github.com/pkziinn10/config-opencode/tree/HEAD/skills/cavekit))

- **cavekit-audit**: Audita en profundidad un dominio (seguridad, privacidad, rendimiento, accesibilidad o cumplimiento): arma un plan exhaustivo, lo revisa contra la app real y lo divide en tareas de solo análisis que escriben un único informe. El análisis largo corre en un subagente y solo vuelve el informe. Usala ante pedidos de auditoría o revisión amplia. NO usarla para una funcionalidad (cavekit-plan + cavekit-build), un bug puntual (cavekit-backprop) ni una revisión de un solo diff (code-review). ([Facundo-Guarnier/guarnold-portfolio](https://github.com/Facundo-Guarnier/guarnold-portfolio/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cavekit-audit) · 2 copies)

([casualjim/pi-mimir](https://github.com/casualjim/pi-mimir/tree/HEAD/packages/pi-cavekit/skills/cavekit-backprop) · ★16)

Usala ante un fallo de test en verificación, un reporte de bug o un incidente en producción. NO usarla para implementar una tarea nueva (cavekit-build) ni para un desvío sin bug (cavekit-check).
